# Dragon Core Requirements Traceability Matrix

- **Document ID:** RCA-DFC-RTM-002
- **Product:** Dragon Core Revision A
- **Organization:** Royal Cubit Aerospace
- **Purpose:** Record exact relationships among system requirements, Architecture contracts, host-visible register fields, RTL ownership, and Verification Plan activities.

## 1. Purpose and Scope

This Requirements Traceability Matrix is a relationship index. It defines no requirement, architecture, register behavior, RTL implementation rule, or verification procedure. Normative content remains in its owning specification under the authority structure defined by `RCA-DFC-CHTR-002`, Section 3.

The master matrix contains each of the 310 requirements in `RCA-DFC-SRS-002` exactly once. Each row identifies only existing Architecture contracts, exact `REGISTER.FIELD` names or `N/A`, exact RTL module owners, exact Verification Plan method codes, and existing unique Verification Plan case identifiers.

## 2. Traceability Rules

1. Requirement wording is not repeated or paraphrased in this document.
2. A matrix relationship does not strengthen, weaken, specialize, or replace the content of either endpoint.
3. `N/A` in the Register fields column means the requirement has no direct host-visible field; it does not mean the requirement lacks observable verification evidence.
4. RTL-024 appears only where it owns top-level integration, wrapper behavior, SPI transport, pad adaptation, centralized buffering, production test, or an end-to-end path explicitly requiring that integration ownership.
5. Verification methods and case identifiers are used exactly as defined in `RCA-DFC-VP-002`; no undefined shorthand or locally invented case identifier is permitted.
6. Appendix A and Appendix B are generated, nonnormative reverse views of the same master matrix. They create no additional allocation.

## 3. Matrix Field Definitions

| Field | Definition |
|---|---|
| SRS requirement ID | One exact identifier from `RCA-DFC-SRS-002`. |
| Architecture contract IDs | Existing identifiers from `RCA-DFC-ARCH-002` that implement or constrain the requirement. |
| Register fields or N/A | Exact host-visible `REGISTER.FIELD` names from `RCA-DFC-REG-002`, or `N/A`. |
| Owning RTL modules | Exact modules that own the allocated behavior; this is not a list of incidental signal consumers. |
| VP method codes | Exact method codes defined by `RCA-DFC-VP-002`, Section 5. |
| Verification-case IDs | Existing unique case identifiers defined by `RCA-DFC-VP-002`, Sections 9 through 25. |

## 4. Requirement Population

| SRS category | Requirements |
|---|---:|
| FUNC — Functional | 132 |
| INTF — External interface | 60 |
| CONF — Configuration and protection | 35 |
| PERF — Performance and timing | 26 |
| SAFE — Safety | 23 |
| REL — Reliability and fault tolerance | 16 |
| ENV — Environmental and physical integration | 6 |
| SEC — Protected access, test, and diagnostics | 12 |
| **Total** | **310** |
